- **armarium**: Personal knowledge keeper over the owner's note base. Recon mode — thematic scan/digest of priority topics, linking findings to saved notes. QA mode — answers "what have I written / thought about X" strictly from the base, grounded with dated citations. Evolution mode — how the owner's view on a topic shifted over time. Maintenance mode — base-health audit (contradictions, duplicates, stale notes, missing links) proposed under a per-item owner-approval gate. Also calibration of forecasts, selection-quality metrics readout, and selection-logic regression checks. Use for "digest / scan / what's… ([KirKruglov/ARMARIUM](https://github.com/KirKruglov/ARMARIUM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armarium.md) · ★1)
- **armature-inventor**: Frontier-robotics researcher — recent papers, novel mechanisms, unusual actuators and materials, filtered against the project's physics into an innovation brief at docs/research/. Dispatch when a design is stuck or conventional, or a trade study's requirements are unusually hard. One idea family per run (mechanism, actuation, material, sensing, manufacturing method), two or three runs at a time, each prompt carrying the design tension in one sentence, the constraint numbers, and what is already ruled out. ([rioharper/armature](https://github.com/rioharper/armature/blob/HEAD/agents/inventor.md))
- **armature-librarian**: Datasheet and OTS-model hunter for robotics parts — finds the document, verifies the exact part number, caches it with provenance in docs/datasheets/ and cad/ots-parts/, and stages its numbers as ledger entries. Dispatch when a decision needs a datasheet number, a list price, or a vendor CAD model the project lacks. Give the exact P/N (cached this run), or a description plus the numbers the decision needs (returned as a candidate for the user to confirm). One part or one slice of a field per run, two or three runs at a time. ([rioharper/armature](https://github.com/rioharper/armature/blob/HEAD/agents/librarian.md))
- **armature-red-team**: Adversarial reviewer for robotics artifacts (specs, derivations, plans, BOMs, part definitions), fresh context by construction — findings report to docs/reviews/, each fix routed to its owner. Dispatch it for every review of an artifact the user asks for; whenever an artifact is drafted or finished; and always before CAD hours or purchases. Commit first, then pass the commit, the artifact paths, the decision the review gates, any risk already accepted, and the report path and format the user fixed. A recheck passes the review, the finding ids and the fix diff. ([rioharper/armature](https://github.com/rioharper/armature/blob/HEAD/agents/red-team.md))
- **Armin**: Smart infrastructure — cost-effective solutions, clever architecture, resource-efficient design patterns ([tmcleod3/voidforge](https://github.com/tmcleod3/voidforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armin-clever.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **armitage**: Reviews against mission criteria, coordinates review team, submits PR review via MOTHER ([zoobz-io/cicero](https://github.com/zoobz-io/cicero/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armitage.md))
- **armory-catalog-auditor**: Audits the health of the whole Armory catalog after a nightly refresh or before a release. Use it when counts look wrong, the site shows stale data, or the lint fails: it checks the index against the brain, the one timestamp, broken related links and stale rows. It reports; it does not change data. ([naman10parikh/armory](https://github.com/naman10parikh/armory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armory-catalog-auditor.md))
- **armory-curator**: Reviews candidate components before they enter Armory, one by one or a whole incoming/ batch. Use it when a crawl or a submission landed new rows and someone must check provenance, licence, duplicates and the frontmatter contract before promotion. It reports; it does not promote or edit. ([naman10parikh/armory](https://github.com/naman10parikh/armory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/armory-curator.md))

Use para mapear um sistema de terceiro e reconstruí-lo com a mesma experiência. É o par do `arq-info` (que documenta o nosso próprio código), mirando um alvo web em vez do repositório. - **Arte**: 1. **Restrição é ferramenta criativa.** Impacto máximo com elementos mínimos. 2. **Máximo 5 cores no sistema.** Primária, secundária, acento, neutro escuro, neutro claro. Mais que isso dilui identidade. 3. **Máximo 2 famílias tipográficas.** Uma para hierarquia principal, outra para corpo ou contraste. 4. **Hierarquia explícita antes de execução.** Toda composição tem leitura principal, secundária ([Yoooobe/landing](https://github.com/Yoooobe/landing/blob/HEAD/protegido-ai-assistant/frameworks/Human%20Team/squads/team/agents/arte.agent.md))
- **arte-conferente**: Confere cada leva de arte ANTES da instalação: alfa real (sem xadrez assado), paleta (sem magenta/roxo/rosa), dimensão e proporção exatas, chão em 74% / 26%, costura de tiles, células de spritesheet, legibilidade em tamanho real, e monta a folha de contato para o checkpoint do dono. Substitui os seis agentes arte-cenario/-criatura/-fx/-emblema/-marca/-hud-visor (fundidos em 21/09/2026, governança F1) — era o mesmo procedimento com bloco de estilo e destino diferentes. A família `loja` (material da Play: 8 screenshots ×2 idiomas por CAPTURA, feature graphic 1024×500 por COMPOSIÇÃO, recorte og:image) nasceu em 21/09/20… ([HexerVoodoom/Soulmon](https://github.com/HexerVoodoom/Soulmon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arte-gerador.md) · ★1)
- **arte-instalador**: Instala no repo o que a SQUAD-ARTE gerou e o arte-conferente aprovou: copia para src/assets, registra nos mapas (decorArt/dreamArt/itemArt/fxArt/nestArt/attackFxArt/emblemArt/sigilArt/placeholderArt/PET_BACKGROUNDS/dungeonScenes), ajusta guards, sobe CACHE_VERSION, roda os testes e commita por caminho. - **article-cold-reader**: A general reader with no access to the sources who reads one compiled article once, cold, and reports where it stopped making sense — undefined terms, opaque sentences, questions raised and not answered, the first point of confusion — plus a five-sentence explain-back. - **article-compliance-reviewer**: Review a diff, PR, or file list against the project's rules in .claude/rules. Reports violations with file:line and a fix; read-only. ([hybridindie/godot-mcp](https://github.com/hybridindie/godot-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-compliance-reviewer.md) · ★74 · 3 copies)
- **article-content-reviewer**: Review technical articles for factual accuracy, technical correctness, and logical consistency ([shengdabai/Tony-Claude-Code-Skills](https://github.com/shengdabai/Tony-Claude-Code-Skills/blob/HEAD/skills/_collections/claude-code/agents/article-content-reviewer.md) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **article-content-reviewer**: 评估技术深度、论证逻辑和章节结构，识别浅薄段落、逻辑跳跃和结构问题。 ([thesues/artist](https://github.com/thesues/artist/blob/HEAD/article-plugin/agents/article-content-reviewer.md))
- **article-content-reviewer-codex**: 内容审查的 Codex 多样性视角（使用 Codex 提供不同模型的深度+逻辑+结构审查观点） ([thesues/artist](https://github.com/thesues/artist/blob/HEAD/article-plugin/agents/article-content-reviewer-codex.md))
- **article-content-reviewer-hermes**: 内容审查的 Hermes 多样性视角（使用 Nous Hermes Agent 提供第三方模型的深度+逻辑+结构审查观点） ([thesues/artist](https://github.com/thesues/artist/blob/HEAD/article-plugin/agents/article-content-reviewer-hermes.md))
- **article-cover-generator**: SEO Phase 3 step. Runs ONLY when cover.enabled is true — otherwise exits SKIPPED. Generates ONE cover image automatically (OpenAI gpt-image-1), converts it to cover.webp in the article dir, and records the prompt into meta.json (cover.prompt). Project-agnostic; style hints from cover.style. Graceful skip when OPENAI_API_KEY is absent. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-cover-generator.md) · ★1)
- **article-critic**: Adversarially reviews a technical article draft on two axes, whether it is technically correct and whether it reads like a professional tech writer or like AI-generated slop. Verifies claims against primary sources, checks specifics against the assignment brief, runs a mechanical scan for AI tells, and returns PUBLISH, REVISE, or REWRITE with quoted, line-level findings. Use after tech-writer, or whenever the user asks to review, critique, or judge an article draft. Does not rewrite the article. ([1Shubham7/agents](https://github.com/1Shubham7/agents/blob/HEAD/agents/article-critic.md) · ★2)
- **article-diagram-renderer**: 从视觉规划报告中提取 SVG 代码块并落盘为 .svg 文件，供文章引用。 ([thesues/artist](https://github.com/thesues/artist/blob/HEAD/article-plugin/agents/article-diagram-renderer.md))
- **article-domain-reviewer**: 記事の専門領域を検出し、最大3つの専門家ペルソナで公式事実・チーム運用・筆者解釈の境界を一次情報に照らしてreview-onlyで確認する。 ([s977043/my-blog](https://github.com/s977043/my-blog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-domain-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **article-draft-writer**: SEO Phase 3 step. Writes the full article draft from brief + outline + sources, in the project's language and voice. Output is body.md — pure article content, no framework front matter. Each hard fact carries an inline citation [^srcN]. Project-agnostic; reads seo/profile.json. Writes human-sounding prose (voice, burstiness, specifics) — not smooth AI filler. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-draft-writer.md) · ★1)
- **article-editor**: Editor for article pipeline. Consolidates review fixes and compiles final article. ([AngusFu/tech-insider](https://github.com/AngusFu/tech-insider/blob/HEAD/agents/article-editor.md))
- **article-explainer**: 合并译文、事实校验、相关阅读和 SVG 图示，产出最终的中文讲解稿（一稿成文，不迭代）。 ([thesues/artist](https://github.com/thesues/artist/blob/HEAD/article-plugin/agents/article-explainer.md))
- **article-fact-checker**: Step 6/9 of SEO Phase 3 — fact-check gate. Extracts every hard fact / concrete claim from the draft and checks it against sources.json, driven by the project profile's contentRigor. In regulated mode it is a HARD gate (missing/off-whitelist citation = BLOCK); in standard mode it is a LIGHT gate (concrete claims need a named credible source; soft issues = WARN). Output: &lt;articlesDir&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;/fact-check.json. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-fact-checker.md) · ★1)
- **article-fact-checker**: مدقق حقائق مقالات مدونة «زوروا الأحساء» — يفتح كل رابط في المقال ويطابق كل معلومة واقعية بمصدرها حرفاً ومعنى، ويرفض ما بلا سند أو بسند لا يقوله. يستدعيه أمر /article في مرحلة النقد وبعد كل تعديل يمسّ معلومة، وفي وضع «مطابقة النسخة» للتحقق من أن نسخة مترجمة لم تغيّر حقيقة. استخدمه أيضاً عند طلب «دقّق حقائق هذا المقال» أو «تأكد أن الروابط تقول ما نسبناه لها». قراءة فقط، لا يعدّل المقال ولا يبحث عن مادة جديدة (ذلك لـ article-researcher) ولا يحكم على الأسلوب أو القانون. ([eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa](https://github.com/eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-fact-checker.md))
- **article-fetcher**: Fetch a single article from a URL and save it to the media-monitoring archive following date-based folder conventions and the article schema. Use when the user provides an article URL to archive, asks to grab a piece of coverage, or wants to capture an article referenced in conversation. ([danielrosehill/Claude-PR-Media-Work-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Claude-PR-Media-Work-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/article-fetcher.md) · ★7)
- **article-fixer**: Applies the merged findings of the article review panel (rules reviewer, cold reader, skeptic) to one Histmersion article — verifying each finding against the database first, rejecting the ones it cannot confirm, and patching the article with patch_article so that it satisfies the reader (definitions, structure) AND the specialist (distinctions, evidence, statuses) at once. ([histmersion/histmersion](https://github.com/histmersion/histmersion/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/article-fixer.md) · 2 copies)
- **article-formatter**: 外部のMarkdownやテキストをChronicleブログ（AstroPaper）形式に変換する。コンテンツは改変せず、Front matter生成と最小限のフォーマット整形のみ行う。 ([junichiro/chronicle](https://github.com/junichiro/chronicle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-formatter.md))
- **article-html-author**: 確定済み記事 Markdown とカバー画像から html-page-design 準拠のリッチ HTML 記事ページと記事一覧トップ index.html を執筆し、公開元ディレクトリへ出力する。全工程合格時のみ commit→push で GitHub Pages へ公開する。レビューレポートを受け取った場合は /multi-aspect-review の再生成 author として該当 HTML を上書き再執筆する（再生成時は push しない）。本文の事実・主張は改変しない。 ([TomokiIshimine/paper-lens](https://github.com/TomokiIshimine/paper-lens/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-html-author.md) · ★1)
- **article-illustrator**: 文章视觉策划与配图子代理。由工作流导演在 Humanizer 之后、最终事实核查之前的 Stage 11 显式调用。 ([dongbeixiaohuo/writing-agent](https://github.com/dongbeixiaohuo/writing-agent/blob/HEAD/claude-runtime/agents/article-illustrator.md) · ★460 · 3 copies)
- **article-illustrator**: [Subagent] 文章配图师。 负责为文章设计视觉风格，自动生成配图并插入到文章中。 采用 "Type × Style" 双维度设计理论，确保配图既有信息量又有美感。 ([railwise-cn/railwise-ai](https://github.com/railwise-cn/railwise-ai/blob/HEAD/src/asset/skills/writing-agent/agents/article-illustrator.md) · ★3)
- **article-image-generator**: 確定済み記事本文からインフォグラフィック 1 枚の構図・配色・画像内日本語文言を設計し、codex-imagegen スキル経由で PNG を生成・保存して絶対パスを返す。レビューレポートを受け取った場合は /multi-aspect-review の再生成 author としてcover.png を上書き再生成する。article.md は書き換えない。 ([TomokiIshimine/paper-lens](https://github.com/TomokiIshimine/paper-lens/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-image-generator.md) · ★1)
- **article-legal-reviewer**: SEO Phase 3 compliance gate. Runs ONLY when contentRigor === "regulated" AND regulated.legalReview is true — otherwise exits SKIPPED. Verifies the article carries the required disclaimer, grounds the expected key acts, and contains no forbidden product claims. Project-agnostic; all criteria come from the regulated.* block of seo/profile.json. Records its verdict into meta.json. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-legal-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **article-legal-reviewer**: المحكّم القانوني لمقالات مدونة «زوروا الأحساء» — يراجع مسودة مقال من جهة حقوق النشر والاقتباس، والادعاءات عن جهات وأشخاص وأنشطة تجارية، ودقة الصفات والتسميات الرسمية، والخلو من المحظورات ومما يخالف أنظمة النشر في المملكة، ويعيد حكماً (مُجاز / مُجاز بتعديلات / مرفوض) بملاحظات محددة الموضع. يستدعيه أمر /article في مرحلة النقد، أو عند طلب «راجع هذا المقال قانونياً» أو «هل في المقال ما يعرّضنا لمساءلة». قراءة فقط. لا يدقق صحة الأرقام بمصادرها (ذلك لـ article-fact-checker) ولا يراجع عقوداً أو سياسات الموقع. ([eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa](https://github.com/eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-legal-reviewer.md))
- **article-linker**: 内部リンク最適化専門エージェント。以下のタスクで使用する： - article-index.json から last_linked が古い/null の記事を最大10件バッチ処理 - 双方向リンク：対象記事→関連記事 と 関連記事→対象記事 の両方を追加 - article-link-card 形式でH2セクション末尾に挿入（常に最良の5本を維持） - 5本上限到達時は新候補と既存リンクの関連度を比較し、より高いものに入れ替える - article-index.json の existing_internal_links / internal_link_count / last_linked を更新 ([gucchi0421/.claude](https://github.com/gucchi0421/.claude/blob/HEAD/__BK__/agents/article-linker.md))
- **article-linker**: 内部リンク最適化専門エージェント（v2）。以下のタスクで使用する： - article-index.json から last_linked が古い/null の記事を最大10件バッチ処理 - 双方向リンク：対象記事→関連記事 と 関連記事→対象記事 の両方を追加 - article-link-card 形式でH2セクション末尾に挿入（常に最良の5本を維持） - 5本上限到達時は新候補と既存リンクの関連度を比較し、より高いものに入れ替える - article-index.json の existing_internal_links / internal_link_count / last_linked を更新 ([gucchi0421/.claude](https://github.com/gucchi0421/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/article-linker.md))
- **article-literary-reviewer**: المحكّم الأدبي لمقالات مدونة «زوروا الأحساء» — يراجع مسودة مقال عربي من جهة سلامة اللغة والنحو والإملاء، وجمال الأسلوب وتماسك البناء، والخلو من الحشو والتكرار والعبارات المستهلكة، ويعيد حكماً (مُجاز / مُجاز بتعديلات / مرفوض) بملاحظات محددة الموضع. يستدعيه أمر /article في مرحلة النقد، أو عند طلب «دقّق لغة هذا المقال» أو «هل أسلوب المقال يليق بالموقع». قراءة فقط، لا يعيد كتابة المقال. لا يفحص صحة المعلومات ولا العنوان من جهة الجذب والبحث (ذلك لـ article-fact-checker و article-media-reviewer)، ولا يراجع نصوصاً بغير العربية. ([eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa](https://github.com/eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-literary-reviewer.md))
- **article-maintenance**: Blog article maintenance — tag population, SEO health, cross-references, stale content detection, and broken link checks for {{PERSONAL_DOMAIN}} ([htekdev/copilot-home-assistant](https://github.com/htekdev/copilot-home-assistant/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/article-maintenance.agent.md) · ★9)
- **article-manager**: Owns the wiki's "Articles" category — one post per external article, analysing and introducing it. Give it an article URL and it fetches the piece, extracts the substance, and writes a Korean analysis/introduction post into `_posts/Articles/` following the canonical structure (원문 정보 → TL;DR → 왜 골랐나 → 핵심 내용 → 분석과 인사이트 → 적용 포인트 → 더 읽어보기), with correct front matter, tags, cross-links, and a clean build. It writes the prose and hands the visuals off to the `post-illustrator` specialist by leaving illustration briefs in the draft. Invoke for "이 아티클로 포스트 써줘 &lt;url&gt;", "Articles 포스트 추가", or "analyze/in… ([orchwang/orchwang.github.io](https://github.com/orchwang/orchwang.github.io/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/article-manager.md) · 2 copies)
- **article-media-reviewer**: المحكّم الإعلامي لمقالات مدونة «زوروا الأحساء» — يراجع مسودة مقال من جهة قوة العنوان والمقدمة، ووضوح الزاوية، والفائدة العملية للزائر، والملاءمة للجمهور ولمحركات البحث والنماذج اللغوية، ويعيد حكماً (مُجاز / مُجاز بتعديلات / مرفوض) بملاحظات محددة الموضع. يستدعيه أمر /article في مرحلة النقد، أو عند طلب «هل هذا المقال يُقرأ ويُعثر عليه» أو «قيّم عنوان المقال ومقدمته». قراءة فقط. لا يدقق اللغة ولا صحة المعلومات (ذلك لـ article-literary-reviewer و article-fact-checker)، ولا يُستخدم لتدقيق SEO تقني للموقع أو لصفحات غير المقالات. ([eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa](https://github.com/eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-media-reviewer.md))
- **article-outline-writer**: Step 3/9 of SEO Phase 3. From brief.md + serp-analysis.md, creates a detailed article outline (H2/H3 + content bullets per section), driven by the project profile. Output: &lt;articlesDir&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;/outline.md. Last step before HUMAN GATE 1. ([alexfilatov/seo-suite](https://github.com/alexfilatov/seo-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/article-outline-writer.md) · ★1)
- **article-patroler**: 公開記事のパトロール・自動修正専門エージェント（v2）。以下のタスクで使用する： - article-index.json から未チェック/古い記事を最大15件取得してバッチ処理 - writer の Markdown仕様に基づくHTML構造の検証・修正 - 内部リンクの死活チェック・リンクテキストとタイトルの不一致修正 - article-index.json との乖離チェック（タイトル・slug不一致） - 修正結果を article-index.json の patrolled_at / patrol_issues に記録 ([gucchi0421/.claude](https://github.com/gucchi0421/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/article-patroler.md))
- **article-patroler**: 公開記事のパトロール・自動修正専門エージェント。以下のタスクで使用する： - article-index.json から未チェック/古い記事を最大15件取得してバッチ処理 - writer の Markdown仕様に基づくHTML構造の検証・修正 - 内部リンクの死活チェック・リンクテキストとタイトルの不一致修正 - article-index.json との乖離チェック（タイトル・slug不一致） - 修正結果を article-index.json の patrolled_at / patrol_issues に記録 ([gucchi0421/.claude](https://github.com/gucchi0421/.claude/blob/HEAD/__BK__/agents/article-patroler.md))
- **article-pipeline**: Гонит статью пользователя по 8 стадиям: рисёрч → драфт → фактчек → голос → корректура → редактура → обложка → docx для habr/vc/rbc/linkedin. НЕ пост в TG-канал → tg-post; НЕ глава книги → book-post. ([JHamidun/claude-code-config-pack](https://github.com/JHamidun/claude-code-config-pack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-pipeline.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)
- **article-pipeline-reviewer**: Reviews article pipeline implementation for structural completeness, skill/agent alignment, plugin configuration. ([AngusFu/tech-insider](https://github.com/AngusFu/tech-insider/blob/HEAD/agents/article-pipeline-reviewer.md))
- **article-planner**: propose-articles: propose the articles a research area should have — each a new slug, title, kind and filter; code checks them and a person always approves the plan. ([histmersion/histmersion](https://github.com/histmersion/histmersion/blob/HEAD/agents/article-planner.md))
- **article-planner**: 会話や議論をブログ記事にしたいときに使用。議論を分析してキーポイントを抽出し、タイトル・タグ・詳細なアウトラインを含む記事構成を提案する。 ([junichiro/chronicle](https://github.com/junichiro/chronicle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-planner.md))
- **article-proofreader**: サイト掲載のメディア記事(frontend/app/articles 配下)を校正するエディター。事実・データ整合性、日本語の誤字脱字・表記ゆれ、AIっぽさ(humanizer-ja準拠)、構成・読みやすさ、内部リンク健全性をチェックし、赤入れ(具体的な修正案)を返す。記事を新規作成・大幅編集したときや「記事を校正して」の依頼で使う。 ([byson2562/rowing-api](https://github.com/byson2562/rowing-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-proofreader.md))
- **article-proofreader**: 既存の土木・コンクリ textbook/guide を PDF なしで 5 軸校正する Evaluator。修正はしない。原典照合は textbook-qa。 ([uruhayato373/doboku-note](https://github.com/uruhayato373/doboku-note/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-proofreader.md))
- **article-reader**: Fetch and analyze a web article URL. Extracts key facts, dates, named entities, monetary amounts, and quotes. Use when the main research agent needs deeper analysis of a specific source. ([tosanoob/cc-research](https://github.com/tosanoob/cc-research/blob/HEAD/agents/article-reader.md))
- **article-reader-reviewer**: サイト掲載のメディア記事(frontend/app/articles 配下)を「読み手の体験」の観点でレビューするエディター。想定読者と開く場面を決め、知りたいことに届く順番か・見出しと表だけで分かるか・次に行くページへの導線があるかを見て、優先度順の指摘と具体的な直し案を返す。校正(誤字・事実)は article-proofreader の担当なので扱わない。記事を新規作成・大幅編集したとき、「読者目線でレビューして」の依頼で使う。 ([byson2562/rowing-api](https://github.com/byson2562/rowing-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-reader-reviewer.md))
- **article-related-finder**: 通过 WebSearch 检索与原文主题相关的论文、官方文档、技术博客，为讲解稿提供延伸阅读和背景上下文。 ([thesues/artist](https://github.com/thesues/artist/blob/HEAD/article-plugin/agents/article-related-finder.md))
- **article-researcher**: Собирает актуальные данные о сервисах для статей-подборок. Исследует каждый сервис из списка: цены, возможности, статус. Делает обзор в контексте темы статьи. ([mikeshu2001/content-factory](https://github.com/mikeshu2001/content-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/article-researcher.md) · ★21)
- **article-researcher**: Researcher for article pipeline. Gathers background information, facts, references using Web Search and Web Fetch. Supports web-search / web-fetch / hybrid modes. ([AngusFu/tech-insider](https://github.com/AngusFu/tech-insider/blob/HEAD/agents/article-researcher.md))
- **article-researcher**: باحث مقالات مدونة «زوروا الأحساء» — يجمع مادة مقال واحد من المصادر المقبولة ويعيدها قائمة حقائق، كل حقيقة بمصدرها ورابطها ونص شاهد حرفي. يستدعيه أمر /article في مرحلة البحث، أو عند طلب «ابحث لي عن مادة موثّقة لمقال عن …» أو «وثّق هذه المعلومة لمقال المدونة». لا يكتب المقال ولا يحكم عليه (التحكيم للمحكّمين الأربعة article-*-reviewer و article-fact-checker)، ولا يُستخدم لبحث بيانات بطاقات المعالم. ([eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa](https://github.com/eyad62689-lab/visit-alahsa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/article-researcher.md))

- **Artinis STEP 09 - Code Review**: Step 09 subagent. Reviews the active story implementation (no code changes) using the Session 8 prompt. ([Horschig/MatheMon](https://github.com/Horschig/MatheMon/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/step-09-review.agent.md))
- **Artinis STEP 10 - Verification**: Step 10 subagent. Performs final verification of the active story using the Session 9 prompt. ([Horschig/MatheMon](https://github.com/Horschig/MatheMon/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/step-10-verification.agent.md))
- **Artinis STEP 11 - Cleanup**: Step 11 subagent. Closes the loop for the active story/epic using the Session 10 cleanup prompt; removes only safe temporary artefacts. ([Horschig/MatheMon](https://github.com/Horschig/MatheMon/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/step-11-cleanup.agent.md))
